def _conv_lin(p):
   N=next_fast_len(2*n-1)
   F=rfft(p,N)
   c=irfft(F*F,N)[:2*n-1]
   return c
```

```
def project_simplex(v):
  n=len(v); u=np.sort(v)[::-1]; cssum=cumsum(u); rho=argmax(u - (cssum+1)/np.arange(1,n+1)>0); theta=(cssum[rho]+1)/(rho+1); return np.maximum(v-theta,0)
```

Let's define `_lse_minimize(p0, alpha, max_time, state)`:
```
def _lse_obj(y):
    yc = y - y.mean()
    z = np.clip(yc, -60, 60)
    p = np.exp(z); p/=p.sum()
    c = _conv(p)
    J = c.max()
    # update best state if J < best_j - 1e-15
    exp_terms = np.exp(alpha*(c-J))
    S = exp_terms.sum()
    val = J + np.log(S)/alpha
    w = exp_terms/S
    grad_p = 2.0 * _grad_corr(w,p)
    g = p * (grad_p - grad_p.dot(p))
    return val, g
```
But with clipping at ±60, gradient not exact if y at clip. Instead use `z = y - y.mean()` and rely on no extreme initial. We can cap at [-700,700] but gradient discontinuity. We can set `z = np.clip(yc, -50, 50)` and accept. If z<-745 underflow to0; fine. No overflow. Good. But if L-BFGS makes y with huge values, p at boundaries; gradient for zero entries is zero (p factor). It can escape? Convolution exact:
```
def convolve_square(p):
  F = rfft(p, Nc)
  c = irfft(F*F, Nc)[:m]
  np.maximum(c,0,out=c)
  return c
```
Nc=next_fast_len(m). m=2n-1.
Gradient corr:
```
def grad_corr(w,p):
  w_rev = w[::-1]
  Fp = rfft(p, Ng)
  Fw = rfft(w_rev, Ng)
  conv = irfft(Fp*Fw, Ng)
  seg = conv[m-n : m]  # length n
  h = seg[::-1]
  return h
```
Let's test for n=2, m=3. p=[a,b], w=[w0,w1,w2]. h_0 should p0 w0 + p1 w1? Formula grad_i=2∑ p_j w_{i+j}. For i=0: j=0 p0 w0, j=1 p1 w1. i=1: p0 w1 + p1 w2. Conv p with w_rev [w2,w1,w0]. Length 4.
conv[0]=p0 w2
conv[1]=p0 w1 +p1 w2
conv[2]=p0 w0 +p1 w1
conv[3]=p1 w0
m-n=1 to m=3 indices 1,2,3: [p0w1+p1w2, p0w0+p1w1, p1w0]. Reverse -> [p1w0, p0w0+p1w1, p0w1+p1w2]. That's not h. Wrong orientation.
Let's recalc. We want h_i=∑_{j=0}^{n-1} p_j w_{i+j}. This is cross-correlation. FFT formula: correlation C[l] = ∑_x A[x] B[x+l]. If A=p length n, B=w length m padded. Then C[i] for i=0..n-1. Standard `ifft(fft(A) * conj(fft(B)))` with B padded gives cyclic correlation? For real: corr[k]=∑ A[j] B[j+k] maybe with zero padding length ≥n+m-1. Let's derive using convolution: corr = ifft(conj(fft(A)) * fft(B)): corr[k]=∑ A[j] B[j+k]? With padding. Let's test with small applying `np.correlate(p, w, mode='valid')` returns h_i length n: np.correlate(p,w,'valid') = ∑ p[j] w[j+i]? In numpy, `correlate(a,v,mode='valid')` returns sum a[m+k] v[m]? For a=p length n, v=w length m≥n, valid length m-n+1, not we want n? If a=w length m, v=p length n: `correlate(w,p,'valid')` length m-n+1 =? m=2n-1, n => n+1? Not. Let's just use direct but optimized via convolution maybe.

We need h_i for i=0..n-1, sum j=0..n-1 p_j w_{i+j}. This is a Hankel matrix-vector product. We can implement direct using `np.einsum` with a strided view? Build w_shift matrix? For 10k gradients 5s? Maybe okay. Simpler and less bug:
```
def grad_corr(w,p):
    # h[i] = dot(p, w[i:i+n])
    return np.array([np.dot(p, w[i:i+n]) for i in range(n)])
```
But Python loop 384 per gradient; 100k gradients -> 38.4M Python loop iterations, too slow. Use vectorized matrix multiplication: Build a strided matrix W with rows = w[i:i+n] and do W @ p. `from numpy.lib.stride_tricks import as_strided`: row shape (n,n), strides (w.strides[0], w.strides[0]) if w contiguous. W is (n,n) views. `W.dot(p)` one BLAS gemv in C. Creating as_strided O(1). Great.
```
W = np.lib.stride_tricks.as_strided(w, shape=(n,n), strides=(w.strides[0], w.strides[0]))
h = W @ p
```
Let's test n=2: w rows: [w0,w1], [w1,w2]. W@p = [p0w0+p1w1, p0w1+p1w2] correct. This is O(n^2) in C (147k), fine. No need FFT for gradient. Objective conv via FFT O(n log n). Could also compute c via FFT. Great. We can avoid gradient FFT bugs.

Projection simplex if needed:
```
def proj_simplex(v):
  u=np.sort(v)[::-1]
  cssum=np.cumsum(u)
  inds=np.arange(1,n+1)
  cond=u - (cssum+1)/inds >0
  if not cond.any(): return v? but standard
  rho=cond[::-1].argmax()-1? Simpler use known:
  rho = np.nonzero(cond)[0].max()
  theta=(cssum[rho]+1)/(rho+1)
  return np.maximum(v-theta,0)
```
Need correct: cond indices 0..n-1; rho max satisfying. Yes.

Now LSE gradient wrt p: `grad_p = 2*W @ p` where W rows weights w. Check derivative of max LSE: L =1/α log ∑ e^{α c_k}, ∂L/∂p_i =∑ w_k ∂c_k/∂p_i =∑ w_k 2 p_{k-i} =2∑_k w_k p_{k-i}. Our h_i=∑_j p_j w_{i+j}; same with k=i+j. Good.

We need h_i = ∑_{j=0}^{n-1} p_j w_{i+j}. This is a cross-correlation. We can compute with scipy.fft correlation. `scipy.signal.correlate` may be easy but maybe import not allowed? scipy available; `scipy.signal` part of scipy, likely allowed. But we can use FFT formula.

def upsample(p, N):
  x_old=np.linspace(0,1,len(p)); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,N)
  q=np.interp(x_new,x_old,p); q=max(q,0); q/=q.sum()
```

Now, potential issue: The evaluator's direct `np.convolve` for length 768 is O(589k) operations; exact validation okay. For internal, FFT safe.

Let's think about LP at n=768. Dense matrix 1535x768 ~1.18M; HiGHS time maybe 0.5-2s. We can call few times. For base 384, many calls okay. We can set LP polish only for base/short time for n>768? For n2, use exact subgradient/coordinate instead of LP to avoid slow. Or include a few LP.

Detailed class Skeleton:
```
import numpy as np, time, math
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len
from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize

class _Opt:
  def __init__(self, n, initial_p=None, best_exact_fallback=None):
     self.n=int(n); self.m=2*self.n-1
     self.Nc=next_fast_len(self.m)
     self.Ng=next_fast_len(3*self.n-2)
     self.best_j=inf
     self.best_p=None
     self.best_exact=inf
     self.best_exact_p=None
     if initial_p is not None: p=normalize; self.update_fft(p)
     if fallback: self.exact_update(fallback)
  def normalize(self,p, floor=1e-14): ...
  def conv(self,p): ...
  def corr_gradient(self,w,p): ...

Normalize:
```
def normalize(self, p):
  p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
  if p.size != self.n: # interpolate? For class, assume size n.
  p=np.where(np.isfinite(p),p,0); p=np.clip(p,0.0,1000.0)
  s=p.sum()
  if s < 1e-12: return np.full(self.n,1.0/self.n)
  p=p/s
  # optional floor
  p = p + 1e-15? Adding breaks sum. Use mix:
  f=1e-12
  p=(1-f)*p + f/self.n
  return p
```
Floor 1e-12 may slightly change score but negligible. For exact optimum, maybe zeros allowed; but floor helps log/LP. If optimal has near zero <1e-12, effect negligible. Use 1e-14 to be safer. `f/self.n` sum f; (1-f)p sum 1-f, total1. p_min ~f/n=2.6e-17 for n=384. Could underflow in log? log 2e-17 okay. For n=768 ~1e-18, still >1e-308. Good.

But if p has large values >1000 before normalization? Clip first. Sum then normalize to <=1. Good.

`update_fft`:

  self.best_j=p_j; self.best_score=2*n*p_j; self.best_p=p.copy()

If p_j close but best_exact lower? We'll exact update separately.

But if we normalize inside exact_update, the p stored is normalized. Good.

Need ensure `con` length Ng, and m-n >=0. For n=1, m=1, m-n=0, slice 0:1 ok.

`lse_objective` closure inside `local_lse` to capture alpha and aborter:
```
def f_and_g(y):
   if any nonfinite y: return 1e100, zeros
   yc = y - np.mean(y)
   # If yc huge, shift by max for exp p
   zmax = yc.max()
   p=np.exp(yc - zmax); s=p.sum(); 
   if s <=0 or not finite: return 1e100, zeros
   p = p/s
   c = self.conv(p)
   J = float(c.max())
   if aborter and aborter[0]: pass
   self.update_fft(p,J)
   zval = alpha*(c-J)
   # clip lower to -745 to avoid underflow? np.exp handles underflow to0; if zval<-745, exp0. Could clip to -745 for speed? no need.
   e = np.exp(np.maximum(zval, -745.0))
   s = e.sum()
   if s <=0: s=1; e[0]=1? But max J index e=exp0=1, s>=1.
   val = J + math.log(s)/alpha
   if not finite: return 1e100,...
   w = e/s
   h = self.corr_gradient(w,p)
   grad_p = 2*h
   g = p * (grad_p - float(grad_p.dot(p)))
   # zero sum small
   return float(val), g
```
The `evaluate_sequence` not here.

Potential performance: `np.maximum(zval,-745)` allocates; instead `np.exp(zval, out=?)`? Simpler: `e = np.exp(zval)`; zval <=0, minimum may be -1e6 causing underflow but no warning? Numpy underflow warning maybe? We can suppress with `np.errstate(under='ignore')`. Use:
```
with np.errstate(under='ignore', over='ignore', invalid='ignore'):
   e=np.exp(zval)
```
Since zval finite negative. Good.

Aborter: If aborter[0], we can still compute? To stop quickly, at start `if aborter[0]: return 1e100, zeros`. L-BFGS callback sets aborter and returns True, but if func currently running, can't stop. After callback before next, stop.

def local_lse(self,p0,alloc_s, alphas=None, lift=1e-12):
  alloc_s=float(alloc_s)
  if alloc_s <=1: return
  p=self.normalize(p0)
  self.exact_update(p)
  if alphas is None: alphas = [(20,1),(100,1),(400,1.2),(1500,1.5),(5000,2)] maybe.
  # allocate times proportional to frac of remaining within alloc (not global)
  total_frac=sum(fr for _,fr in alphas)
  p_start=p
  for alpha, frac in alphas:
    phase_alloc=alloc_s*frac/total_frac
    phase_stop=time.monotonic()+phase_alloc
    if phase_stop < time.monotonic()+1: continue
    y=np.log(p_start + 1e-300); y-=y.mean()
    aborter=[False]
    def cb(pk):
       if time.monotonic() > phase_stop - 0.2 or global_deadline? Use phase_stop:
          aborter[0]=True; return True
    try:
      res=minimize(f_and_g, y, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, callback=cb,
                   options={'maxiter': max(200, int(phase_alloc*20)), 'maxls':30, 'ftol':1e-15, 'gtol':1e-9})
    except Exception: pass
    # after phase, update p_start to best_p if exists
    if self.best_p is not None and self.best_p.size==self.n:
       # Use a slight floor for next phase
       p_start=self.normalize(self.best_p)
    else: p_start=self.normalize(res.x if res...)?
    self.exact_update(p_start)
    if self.best_score <= self.target? optional stop.
  return
```

def lp_direction(self,p,c,J, active_limit=250, time_limit=8.0):
  n=self.n; m=self.m
  if np.all(~np.isfinite(p)): return None
  # Build C
  C=np.empty((m,n)); # can't uninitialized? zeros to avoid? We'll assign all rows? For each j, rows j:j+n assigned p; this covers all cells? For m x n, yes each column assigned n contiguous rows; total m*n cells all assigned exactly once? Let's check row 0 col0 p0; row1 col0 p1,col1 p0; ... row m-1 col n-1 p0? Every cell (r,c) assigned if c≤r≤c+n-1; since c 0..n-1 and r 0..2n-2, is every cell covered? For r=0, only c=0. r=1, c=0,1. Yes triangular full. C initialized empty but all assigned? For each column c, rows c..c+n-1 assigned. Union over c covers 0..2n-2. There are n*n cells total? Wait m*n > n^2 (2n-1)*n vs n^2; But C has 2n-1 rows, n cols; total ~2n^2. Each column assigned n cells, total n^2. Only half of matrix assigned! Ah convolution matrix C is sparse; only anti-diagonal bands. For LP, constraints rows r have n - |r-(n-1)|? Actually row r has coefficients p_{r-c} for c max(0,r-n+1)..min(n-1,r); many zeros. We need zeros elsewhere. So initialize zeros and assign.
  C=np.zeros((m,n))
  for col in range(n): C[col:col+n, col]=p
```
This fills n columns each length n = n^2 cells nonzero; rest zero. Good.

def line_search_dir(self,p,q):

def pair_polish(self,p,stop,seed, max_moves=20000):
  rng=default_rng(seed)
  p=self.normalize(p.copy()); J=self.conv(p).max(); self.update_fft(p,J)
  moves=0
  while time<stop:
    i=int(rng.integers(n)); j=int(rng.integers(n))
    if i==j: continue
    oi=p[i]; oj=p[j]; tot=oi+oj
    rvals=(0,0.1,0.3,0.5,0.7,0.9,1.0)
    best_r=0; best_j=J
    for r in rvals:
       p[i]=r*tot; p[j]=(1-r)*tot
       jv=self.conv(p).max()
       if jv<best_j: best_j=jv; best_r=r; best_i? store
    if best_r !=0:
       p[i]=best_r*tot; p[j]=(1-best_r)*tot
       p=self.normalize(p) # sum same but floor? not needed
       J=best_j; update; maybe exact_update occasionally
    else:
       p[i],p[j]=oi,oj
    moves+=1
    if moves%500==0: exact_update(p)
  return p
```

Now, test mathematical details for objective conversion: We keep p sum1. internal max J. exact score =2nJ (subject to rounding). For n2, exact score. Good.

Let's consider possible bug in gradient sign/objective: LSE value = J + logsum(α(c-J))/α. Since c≤J, logsum ≤ log m; val ≥J. Minimize val. Gradient wrt p: weights w_k = e^{α(c_k-J)}/s. ∂val/∂p_i= ∑ w_k ∂c_k/∂p_i =2h_i. For logit y, gy = p*(grad_p - dot(grad_p,p)). Let's verify with a simple 1D constraint: If all y changed by constant, p unchanged; gy should zero sum. dot(grad_p,p)=∑ p_i grad_p_i. gy_i=p_i(grad_p_i - avg). Sum gy = ∑p grad_p - (∑p)avg=0. Good. L-BFGS on y descends LSE? Since gradient wrt y = jac p wrt y^T grad_p. If gy has zero sum, okay. The function is invariant to adding constant; gradient zero for flat direction. Good.

But L-BFGS on invariant function may have singular Hessian; L-BFGS approx might not like it but should. We recenter y each objective. However L-BFGS stores gradients including flat direction zero; okay. It might wander in y constants; finite. We can fix one dimension to remove invariance: optimize y with `sum(y)=0` constraint? Not easy. Or set y_n-1=0 by optimizing n-1 variables? Could implement: use all n but after each `fun` call we can't modify x. Could fix by making last logit = -sum others? More complex. Invariance usually manageable.

Maybe use projected gradient on p to avoid invariance. But L-BFGS likely okay.

Potential issue: `np.exp(yc - yc.max())` with flat direction: if y has NaN due L-BFGS, handle.

Let's implement `f_and_g` robustly:
```
def f_and_g(y, _alpha=alpha):
  if aborter[0]: return 1e100, np.zeros(self.n)
  y=np.asarray(y,float)
  if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)): return 1e100, zeros
  y = y - np.mean(y)
  # if range too large, reduce? ymax=y.max(); y=y-ymax
  z = y - y.max()
  p=np.exp(z); s=p.sum()
  if not finite s or s<1e-300: return 1e100, zeros
  p=p/s
  c=self.conv(p); J=float(c.max())
  self.update_fft(p,J)
  zmat=_alpha*(c-J)
  with np.errstate(under='ignore', over='ignore', invalid='ignore'):
     e=np.exp(zmat)
  s=e.sum()
  if not finite s or s<1e-300:
     e=np.zeros(self.m); e[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0; s=1.0
  val=J + math.log(s)/_alpha
  if not math.isfinite(val): val=1e100
  w=e/s
  h=self.corr_gradient(w,p)
  gp=2*h
  mean=float(gp.dot(p))
  g=p*(gp-mean)
  # Remove tiny imaginary? real
  g=np.where(np.isfinite(g),g,0.0)
  return float(val), g
```
If L-BFGS line search asks val and grad consistent? yes.

For alpha high, `e` may have many zeros; `log(s)` 0; gradient active. If active constraints multiple with equal max, e for all equal (if c-J=0) weight uniform over maxima. Good.

Let's write `lp_polish` more concretely:
```
def lp_polish(self,p,stop,seed=123, max_iter=200):
  p=self.normalize(p)
  c=self.conv(p); J=float(c.max()); self.update_fft(p,J); self.exact_update(p)
  rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
  stall=0; last_exact=None
  for it in range(max_iter):
    if time.monotonic() > stop - 2: break
    if self.best_score < 1.500 and stop? maybe continue
    # active selection based on current c,J
    act_tol = max(1e-12, 1e-7*J)
    active=np.where(c >= J - act_tol)[0]
    if len(active)==0: active=np.argsort(-c)[:min(50,self.m)]
    # Try different active limits? first all/top, then top 50 if stall
    active_limit = 400 if self.n<=512 else 200
    q,tlp=self.lp_direction(p,c,J,active,active_limit,time_limit=min(8,max(1,(stop-time.monotonic())/10)))
    if q is None:
       stall+=1
       if stall>5: break
       # small smooth perturbation
       p=self.normalize(p + 0.01*self.smooth_noise(rng))
       c=self.conv(p); J=float(c.max()); self.update_fft(p,J); continue
    res=self.line_search_dir(p,q)
    if res is None:
       stall+=1
       if stall>8: break
       # try shorter/active top smaller? maybe just perturb
       continue
    pn,Jn= res
    if Jn < J - 1e-14:
       p=pn; J=Jn; c=self.conv(p); self.update_fft(p,J);
       if it%3==0 or Jn < self.best_j - 1e-10: self.exact_update(p)
       stall=0
    else:
       stall+=1
       if stall>=10:
          # yield to LSE-like small random
          break
  self.exact_update(p)
  return p
```

freq=np.arange(self.Ng) # for rfft length, frequency bin approx; cutoff small
mask = 1/(1+(freq/self.n*0.5)**2)
noise=irfft(F*mask, self.Ng)[:self.n]
return noise
```

  except Exception:
    h=None
  if h is None:
     return np.ones(384)

     a=np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
  except Exception:
     return np.ones(384)
  if a.size==0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
     a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
     if a.size==0: return np.ones(384)

```
def _make_inits(n, parent, seed):
  rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
  out=[]
  def add(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,float).ravel()
    if p.size!=n: ...
    p=np.clip(p,0.0,1000.0)
    if p.sum()>1e-12: out.append(p)
  parr=_normalize(parent,n)
  add(parr); add(parr[::-1])
  t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
  i=np.arange(n)+1
  for a in (-5,-2.5,-1.2,-0.6,-0.3,0.3,0.6,1.2,2.5,5): add(np.exp(a*t))
  for g in (-1.0,-0.6,-0.3,0,0.3?,0.6,1,2): 
     p=i**g
     add(p); add(p[::-1])
  for k in (1,2,3,4,6):
    for amp in (0.4,0.8):
      for ph in (0,0.7):
        add(0.1 + (1+amp*np.cos(2*math.pi*k*t + ph)).clip(0,None))
  # random smooth exp
  Ng=next_fast_len(3*n)
  freq=np.arange(Ng)
  for s in range(8):
    x=rng.normal(0,1,n)
    F=rfft(x,Ng)
    mask=1.0/(1.0+(freq/(n/3.0))**2)
    x=irfft(F*mask,Ng)[:n]
    add(np.exp(0.8*x))
  # parent lowpass noise
  for s in range(6):
    x=rng.normal(0,1,n)
    F=rfft(x,Ng); mask=1.0/(1.0+(freq/(n/4.0))**2); x=irfft(F*mask,Ng)[:n]
    for sc in (0.2,0.5): add(parent*(1.0+sc*x))
  # two-level? maybe p = parent + amplitude*abs(random)
  # zip? maybe skip
  return out
```
Filtering: ensure positive. For cos with 0.1+ (1+amp cos) if amp >1 can be negative; clip.

`_lowpass_noise(rng,n,Ng,freq,bw)`:
```
def _lpnoise(rng,n,Ng,freq,width):
  x=rng.normal(0,1,n)
  F=rfft(x,Ng)
  mask=1.0/(1.0+(freq/width)**2)
  return irfft(F*mask,Ng)[:n]
```

def smooth_noise(self, rng, scale=1.0, width=None):
  if width is None: width=max(4,self.n/4.0)
  x=rng.normal(0,1,self.n)
  Ng=self.Ng
  freq=np.arange(Ng)
  mask=1.0/(1.0+(freq/width)**2)
  F=rfft(x,Ng)
  y=irfft(F*mask,Ng)[:self.n]
  return y
```

Now, coordinate pair polish: Use `self.conv` each allocation. If `run` uses `p` original and resets? We'll implement carefully:
```
def pair_polish(self,p,stop,seed=777,max_moves=50000):
  p=self.normalize(p.copy())
  J=float(self.conv(p).max()); self.update_fft(p,J); self.exact_update(p)
  rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
  moves=0
  ratios=(0.0,0.1,0.25,0.4,0.5,0.6,0.75,0.9,1.0)
  while time.monotonic() < stop-3 and moves<max_moves:
     i=int(rng.integers(self.n)); j=int(rng.integers(self.n))
     if i==j: continue
     oi=p[i]; oj=p[j]; tot=oi+oj
     if tot<1e-14:
       moves+=1; continue
     best_r=None; best_j=J
     best_alter=None
     for r in ratios:
        p[i]=r*tot; p[j]=(1-r)*tot
        jv=float(self.conv(p).max())
        if jv < best_j - 1e-15:
           best_j=jv; best_r=r; best_alter=(r*tot,(1-r)*tot)
     if best_r is not None:
        p[i],p[j]=best_alter
        J=best_j; self.update_fft(p,J); moves+=1
        if moves%300==0: self.exact_update(p)
     else:
        p[i],p[j]=oi,oj
        moves+=1
  self.exact_update(p)
  return p
```
This may not renormalize sum (sum remains1 but floating). Good.

Use pair polish at end for base and double if time. It can improve slightly.

Now, possible modification: The evaluator's score is invariant under scaling but if p sum1, exact score=2n max b; internal J. For pair polish, if sum drifts due floating, small; we can normalize occasionally. But sum preserving exactly? Floating addition may drift; after 50k moves, maybe 1e-14. Fine. At end normalize.

4. Base local tasks with time allocation.
5. Base LP polish and pair polish if time.
6. Double length if time and n2 <= maybe 1024.
7. Final choose best_score/best_seq; if None, return [1.0].

       nonlocal loc_best_p, loc_best_j
       ...

Class methods code:
```
class _Opt:
    def __init__(self,n):
        self.n=int(n)
        self.m=2*self.n-1
        self.Nc=next_fast_len(self.m)
        self.Ng=next_fast_len(3*self.n-2)
        self.freq=np.arange(self.Ng)
        self.best_j=math.inf
        self.best_score=math.inf
        self.best_p=None
        self.best_exact=math.inf
        self.best_exact_p=None
```
`freq` for smooth noise.

    def normalize(self,p):

```

`conv`:
```
    def conv(self,p):
        p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size != self.n:
            p=_normalize(p,self.n)
        F=rfft(p,self.Nc)
        c=irfft(F*F,self.Nc)[:self.m]
        if c.size:
            np.maximum(c,0.0,out=c)
        return c
```
If `irfft` returns array with tiny imaginary? real.

`corr_gradient`:
```
    def corr_gradient(self,w,p):
        w=np.asarray(w,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        wr=w[::-1]
        Fp=rfft(p,self.Ng)
        Fw=rfft(wr,self.Ng)
        c=irfft(Fp*Fw,self.Ng)
        h=c[self.m-self.n:self.m][::-1]
        return h
```
Maybe `h` length n. Good.

`update_fft`:
```
    def update_fft(self,p,p_j=None):
        p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size != self.n: p=self.normalize(p)
        if p_j is None:
            p_j=float(self.conv(p).max())
        if math.isfinite(p_j) and p_j < self.best_j - 1e-15:
            self.best_j=p_j
            self.best_score=2.0*self.n*p_j
            self.best_p=p.copy()
```
If p from loc_best improves local but not global, update_fft does nothing; okay.

`local_lse` full:
```
    def local_lse(self,p0,alloc_s,deadline,alphas=None):
        alloc_s=float(alloc_s)
        alloc_s=min(alloc_s, max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 30.0))
        if alloc_s < 10.0: return
        p=self.normalize(p0)
        J0=float(self.conv(p).max())
        self.update_fft(p,J0)
        self.exact_update(p)
        loc_p=p.copy(); loc_j=J0
        if alphas is None:
            alphas=((30,1.0),(100,1.0),(350,1.2),(1200,1.5),(4000,2.0))
        total=sum(fr for _,fr in alphas)
        REG=1e-10
        for alpha,frac in alphas:
            phase_alloc=alloc_s*frac/total
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 35: break
            if phase_alloc < 5.0: continue
            phase_stop=min(deadline-30.0, time.monotonic()+phase_alloc)
            p_start=self.normalize(loc_p)
            y=np.log(p_start + 1e-300)
            y=float? y=y-y.mean()
            abort=[False]
            def f_and_g(y, _alpha=alpha):
                nonlocal loc_p, loc_j
                if abort[0]:
                    return 1e100, np.zeros(self.n)
                yy=np.asarray(y,dtype=np.float64)
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(yy)):
                    return 1e100, np.zeros(self.n)
                yc=yy-yy.mean()
                # optional: if max abs too big, clip? Use stable softmax directly.
                zm=yc-yc.max()
                with np.errstate(over='ignore', under='ignore', invalid='ignore'):
                    pe=np.exp(zm)
                s=pe.sum()
                if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
                    return 1e100, np.zeros(self.n)
                pp=pe/s
                c=self.conv(pp)
                J=float(c.max())
                if math.isfinite(J) and J < loc_j - 1e-15:
                    loc_j=J; loc_p=pp.copy()
                    self.update_fft(pp,J)
                diff=_alpha*(c-J)
                with np.errstate(under='ignore', over='ignore', invalid='ignore'):
                    e=np.exp(diff)
                se=e.sum()
                if not math.isfinite(se) or se <= 0.0:
                    e=np.zeros(self.m); e[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0; se=1.0
                val=J + math.log(se)/_alpha + REG*float(yc.dot(yc))
                if not math.isfinite(val): val=1e100
                w=e/se
                h=self.corr_gradient(w,pp)
                gp=2.0*h
                mean=float(gp.dot(pp))
                g=pp*(gp-mean) + 2.0*REG*yc
                g=np.where(np.isfinite(g),g,0.0)
                return float(val), g
            def cb(it):
                if time.monotonic() > phase_stop - 0.25:
                    abort[0]=True
                    return True
                return None
            res=None
            try:
                res=minimize(f_and_g, y, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, callback=cb,
                             options={'maxiter': max(50, int(phase_alloc*30)),
                                      'maxls': 30,
                                      'ftol': 1e-15,
                                      'gtol': 1e-9})
            except Exception:
                res=None
            if res is not None and getattr(res,'x',None) is not None:
                pr=self.normalize(res.x)
                jr=float(self.conv(pr).max())
                if jr < loc_j - 1e-15:
                    loc_j=jr; loc_p=pr; self.update_fft(pr,jr)
            self.update_fft(loc_p, loc_j)
            if best_score? No access; just update solver.
        self.update_fft(loc_p, loc_j)
        self.exact_update(loc_p)
        return loc_p
```
In `f_and_g`, `nonlocal loc_p, loc_j` works. But `loc_p` assigned in loop? It is local to method, yes. `loc_j` float. Good.

One subtlety: `yc` used in gradient REG; but `yc` is the centered y input. If `f_and_g` called during line search with y at bounds? no bounds.

`math.isfinite(se)` where se numpy scalar; works.

`maxiter`: If phase_alloc 100s, int*30=3000. Good.

Now `lp_polish` method code:
```
    def smooth_noise(self,rng,width=None):
        if width is None: width=max(4.0,self.n/4.0)
        x=rng.normal(0.0,1.0,self.n)
        F=rfft(x,self.Ng)
        mask=1.0/(1.0+(self.freq/width)**2)
        y=irfft(F*mask,self.Ng)[:self.n]
        return y

    def build_C(self,p):
        C=np.zeros((self.m,self.n), dtype=np.float64)
        for col in range(self.n):
            C[col:col+self.n, col] = p
        return C

    def lp_direction(self,p,c,J,active,active_limit,time_limit):
         ...
```

```

        if m_act == 0: active_top=np.argsort(-c)[:min(50,self.m)]; ...

            # set already top but pass

        except Exception: return None

```
    def line_search_dir(self,p,q):
        p=np.asarray(p); q=np.asarray(q)
        J0=float(self.conv(p).max())
        best_j=J0; best_pn=None
        alphas=np.unique(np.concatenate((np.geomspace(1e-6,1.0,45), np.linspace(0.01,1.0,45))))
        dq=q-p
        for a in alphas:
            if a <=0: continue
            pn = p + a*dq
            # if a>1? no
            pn = np.maximum(pn,0.0)
            s=pn.sum()
            if s <= 1e-15: continue
            pn=pn/s
            j=float(self.conv(pn).max())
            if j < best_j - 1e-15:
                best_j=j; best_pn=pn.copy()
        if best_pn is None: return None
        return best_pn,best_j
```

`_normalize` uses interpolation if p.size != n. If p.size=0, maybe returns ones. Code:
```
def _normalize(p,n,floor=1e-14):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size != n:
       if p.size == 0:
          p=np.ones(n,float)
       else:
          x0=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,p.size)
          x1=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
          p=np.interp(x1,x0,np.clip(p,0.0,1000.0))
    p=np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p=np.clip(p,0.0,1000.0)
    s=float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
       return np.full(n,1.0/n)
    p=p/s
    if floor > 0.0:
       p=(1.0-floor)*p + floor/n
    return p
```
If p has very large values but finite, interpolation okay. If p sum huge, normalize. If p has all zero, uniform. Good.

```
def _make_inits(n, parent, seed):
    rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
    out=[]
    norm_parent=_normalize(parent,n)
    def add(p):
       p=np.asarray(p,float).ravel()
       if p.size!=n: p=_normalize(p,n)
       p=np.where(np.isfinite(p),p,0)
       p=np.clip(p,0.0,1000.0)
       if p.sum() > 1e-12:
           out.append(p)
    add(norm_parent); add(norm_parent[::-1])
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    i=np.arange(n)+1
    ...
    Ng=next_fast_len(3*n)
    freq=np.arange(Ng)
    def lp(rng,bw):
       x=rng.normal(0,1,n)
       F=rfft(x,Ng)
       mask=1.0/(1.0+(freq/bw)**2)
       return irfft(F*mask,Ng)[:n]
    for s in range(8): add(np.exp(0.8*lp(rng,n/3.0)))
    for s in range(6):
       x=lp(rng,n/4.0)
       add(norm_parent*(1.0+0.2*x)); add(norm_parent*(1.0+0.5*x))
    # add parent with endpoint boost
    add(norm_parent * (0.5 + np.linspace(0.5,1.5,n)))
    add(norm_parent * (1.5 - np.linspace(0.5,1.5,n)))
    return out
```

def _upsample(p,N):

    x0=np.linspace(0,1,p.size); x1=np.linspace(0,1,N)

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.fft import rfft, irfft, next_fast_len
from scipy.optimize import linprog, minimize


def _get_parent():
    h = None
    try:
        h = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
    except Exception:
        h = None
    if h is None:
        return np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64)
    try:
        a = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if a.size == 0 or float(a.sum()) <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(384, dtype=np.float64)
    return a


def _normalize(p, n, floor=1e-14):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size != n:
        if p.size == 0:
            p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, p.size)
            x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
            p = np.interp(x1, x0, np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0))
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return np.full(int(n), 1.0 / int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    p = p / s
    if floor > 0.0:
        p = (1.0 - floor) * p + (floor / int(n))
    return p


def _upsample(p, N):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size <= 1:
        return _normalize(p, int(N))
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, p.size)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(N))
    q = np.interp(x1, x0, np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0))
    return _normalize(q, int(N))


def _make_inits(n, parent, seed):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    out = []
    par = _normalize(parent, n)

    def add(p):
        p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size != n:
            p = _normalize(p, n)
        p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if p.size == n and float(p.sum()) > 1e-12:
            out.append(p)

    add(par)
    add(par[::-1])

    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 1.0

    for a in (-5.0, -2.5, -1.2, -0.6, -0.3, 0.3, 0.6, 1.2, 2.5, 5.0):
        add(np.exp(a * t))

    for g in (-1.0, -0.6, -0.3, 0.0, 0.3, 0.6, 1.0, 2.0):
        p = i ** g
        add(p)
        add(p[::-1])

    for k in (1, 2, 3, 4, 6):
        for amp in (0.4, 0.8):
            for ph in (0.0, 0.7):
                add(np.maximum(0.05 + (1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * k * t + ph)), 0.0))

    Ng = next_fast_len(3 * n)
    freq = np.arange(Ng)

    def lp(bw):
        x = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
        F = rfft(x, Ng)
        mask = 1.0 / (1.0 + (freq / max(2.0, bw)) ** 2)
        return irfft(F * mask, Ng)[:n]

    for _ in range(8):
        add(np.exp(0.8 * lp(max(4.0, n / 3.0))))

    for _ in range(4):
        x = lp(max(4.0, n / 4.0))
        add(par * (1.0 + 0.20 * x))
        add(par * (1.0 + 0.50 * x))

    add(par * (0.5 + np.linspace(0.5, 1.5, n)))
    add(par * (1.5 - np.linspace(0.5, 1.5, n)))

    return out


class _Opt:
    def __init__(self, n):
        self.n = int(n)
        self.m = 2 * self.n - 1
        self.Nc = next_fast_len(self.m)
        self.Ng = next_fast_len(3 * self.n - 2)
        self.freq = np.arange(self.Ng)
        self.best_j = math.inf
        self.best_score = math.inf
        self.best_p = None
        self.best_exact = math.inf
        self.best_exact_p = None

    def normalize(self, p):
        return _normalize(p, self.n)

    def conv(self, p):
        p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size != self.n:
            p = self.normalize(p)
        F = rfft(p, self.Nc)
        c = irfft(F * F, self.Nc)[:self.m]
        if c.size:
            np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)
        return c

    def corr_gradient(self, w, p):
        w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        wr = w[::-1]
        Fp = rfft(p, self.Ng)
        Fw = rfft(wr, self.Ng)
        c = irfft(Fp * Fw, self.Ng)
        return c[self.m - self.n:self.m][::-1]

    def update_fft(self, p, p_j=None):
        p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size != self.n:
            p = self.normalize(p)
        if p_j is None:
            p_j = float(self.conv(p).max())
        if math.isfinite(p_j) and p_j < self.best_j - 1e-15:
            self.best_j = p_j
            self.best_score = 2.0 * self.n * p_j
            self.best_p = p.copy()

    def exact_update(self, p):
        if p is None:
            return
        p = self.normalize(p.copy())
        try:
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in p]))
        except Exception:
            sc = math.inf
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < self.best_exact - 1e-12:
            self.best_exact = sc
            self.best_exact_p = p.copy()

    def smooth_noise(self, rng, width=None):
        if width is None:
            width = max(4.0, self.n / 4.0)
        x = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, self.n)
        F = rfft(x, self.Ng)
        mask = 1.0 / (1.0 + (self.freq / max(2.0, width)) ** 2)
        return irfft(F * mask, self.Ng)[:self.n]

    def build_C(self, p):
        C = np.zeros((self.m, self.n), dtype=np.float64)
        for col in range(self.n):
            C[col:col + self.n, col] = p
        return C

    def local_lse(self, p0, alloc_s, deadline, alphas=None):
        alloc_s = float(alloc_s)
        alloc_s = min(alloc_s, max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic() - 30.0))
        if alloc_s < 10.0:
            return self.normalize(p0)

        p = self.normalize(p0)
        J0 = float(self.conv(p).max())
        self.update_fft(p, J0)
        self.exact_update(p)

        loc_p = p.copy()
        loc_j = J0

        if alphas is None:
            scale = max(0.5, min(8.0, float(self.n) / 384.0))
            alphas = (
                (30.0 * scale, 1.0),
                (100.0 * scale, 1.0),
                (350.0 * scale, 1.2),
                (1200.0 * scale, 1.5),
                (4000.0 * scale, 2.0),
            )

        total = sum(fr for _, fr in alphas)
        REG = 1e-10

        for alpha, frac in alphas:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 35.0:
                break
            phase_alloc = alloc_s * frac / total
            if phase_alloc < 5.0:
                continue
            phase_stop = min(deadline - 30.0, time.monotonic() + phase_alloc)

            p_start = self.normalize(loc_p)
            y = np.log(p_start + 1e-300)
            y = y - y.mean()
            abort = [False]

            def f_and_g(y, _alpha=alpha):
                nonlocal loc_p, loc_j
                if abort[0]:
                    return 1e100, np.zeros(self.n)
                yy = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64)
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(yy)):
                    return 1e100, np.zeros(self.n)
                yc = yy - yy.mean()
                zm = yc - yc.max()
                with np.errstate(over="ignore", under="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
                    pe = np.exp(zm)
                s = float(pe.sum())
                if not math.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
                    return 1e100, np.zeros(self.n)
                pp = pe / s

                c = self.conv(pp)
                J = float(c.max())
                if math.isfinite(J) and J < loc_j - 1e-15:
                    loc_j = J
                    loc_p = pp.copy()
                    self.update_fft(pp, J)

                diff = _alpha * (c - J)
                with np.errstate(under="ignore", over="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
                    e = np.exp(diff)
                se = float(e.sum())
                if not math.isfinite(se) or se <= 0.0:
                    e = np.zeros(self.m)
                    e[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
                    se = 1.0

                val = J + math.log(se) / _alpha + REG * float(yc.dot(yc))
                if not math.isfinite(val):
                    val = 1e100

                w = e / se
                h = self.corr_gradient(w, pp)
                gp = 2.0 * h
                mean = float(gp.dot(pp))
                g = pp * (gp - mean) + 2.0 * REG * yc
                g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
                return float(val), g

            def cb(it):
                if time.monotonic() > phase_stop - 0.25:
                    abort[0] = True
                    return True
                return None

            res = None
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    f_and_g,
                    y,
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    jac=True,
                    callback=cb,
                    options={
                        "maxiter": max(50, int(phase_alloc * 30)),
                        "maxls": 30,
                        "ftol": 1e-15,
                        "gtol": 1e-9,
                    },
                )
            except Exception:
                res = None

            if res is not None and getattr(res, "x", None) is not None:
                pr = self.normalize(res.x)
                jr = float(self.conv(pr).max())
                if jr < loc_j - 1e-15:
                    loc_j = jr
                    loc_p = pr
                    self.update_fft(pr, jr)

            self.update_fft(loc_p, loc_j)

        self.update_fft(loc_p, loc_j)
        self.exact_update(loc_p)
        return loc_p

    def line_search_dir(self, p, q):
        p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        q = np.asarray(q, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size != self.n or q.size != self.n:
            return None
        J0 = float(self.conv(p).max())
        best_j = J0
        best_pn = None
        dq = q - p
        alphas = np.geomspace(1e-6, 1.0, 35)
        alphas = np.concatenate((alphas, np.linspace(0.01, 1.0, 25)))
        for a in alphas:
            if a <= 0.0:
                continue
            pn = p + float(a) * dq
            pn = np.maximum(pn, 0.0)
            s = float(pn.sum())
            if s <= 1e-15:
                continue
            pn = pn / s
            j = float(self.conv(pn).max())
            if j < best_j - 1e-15:
                best_j = j
                best_pn = pn.copy()
        if best_pn is None:
            return None
        return best_pn, best_j

    def lp_direction(self, p, c, J, active, active_limit, time_limit):
        p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if p.size != self.n:
            p = self.normalize(p)
        c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if c.size != self.m:
            c = self.conv(p)
        J = float(J)
        if not math.isfinite(J) or J <= 0.0:
            return None
        active = np.asarray(active, dtype=int)
        active = active[(active >= 0) & (active < self.m)]
        if active.size == 0:
            active = np.argsort(-c)[:min(50, self.m)]
        if active.size > active_limit:
            active = np.argsort(-c)[:active_limit]
        if active.size == 0:
            return None

        C = self.build_C(p)
        m_act = int(active.size)
        A = np.zeros((m_act + self.m, self.n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
        A[:m_act, :self.n] = C[active]
        A[:m_act, self.n] = -1.0
        A[m_act:, :self.n] = C
        b = np.concatenate((np.zeros(m_act, dtype=np.float64), np.full(self.m, J, dtype=np.float64)))
        cobj = np.zeros(self.n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        cobj[-1] = 1.0
        Aeq = np.zeros((1, self.n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
        Aeq[0, :self.n] = 1.0
        bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * self.n + [(0.0, max(1e-300, J))]

        try:
            res = linprog(
                cobj,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=b,
                A_eq=Aeq,
                b_eq=[1.0],
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
                options={"presolve": True, "time_limit": max(0.5, float(time_limit))},
            )
        except Exception:
            return None

        if res is None or res.x is None:
            return None
        status = getattr(res, "status", 1)
        if status not in (0, 3):
            return None

        q = np.asarray(res.x[:self.n], dtype=np.float64)
        tlp = float(res.x[self.n])
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(q)):
            return None
        q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
        s = float(q.sum())
        if s <= 0.0:
            return None
        q = q / s
        return q, tlp

    def lp_polish(self, p, stop, seed=2024, max_iter=90):
        if self.n > 850:
            return self.normalize(p)
        p = self.normalize(p.copy())
        c = self.conv(p)
        J = float(c.max())
        self.update_fft(p, J)
        self.exact_update(p)
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        stall = 0
        active_limit = 300 if self.n <= 512 else 160

        for it in range(max_iter):
            if time.monotonic() > stop - 5.0:
                break
            tol = max(1e-12, 1e-7 * J)
            active = np.where(c >= J - tol)[0]
            if active.size == 0:
                active = np.argsort(-c)[:min(60, self.m)]
            if it % 4 == 3:
                active = np.argsort(-c)[:min(self.m, max(active_limit + 100, self.m // 2))]

            time_left = max(0.0, stop - time.monotonic())
            tl = min(10.0, max(0.5, time_left / 8.0))
            q, tlp = self.lp_direction(p, c, J, active, active_limit, tl)

            if q is None or not math.isfinite(tlp):
                stall += 1
                if stall > 6:
                    break
                noise = 0.02 * self.smooth_noise(rng)
                p = self.normalize(p * (1.0 + noise))
                c = self.conv(p)
                J = float(c.max())
                self.update_fft(p, J)
                continue

            if tlp >= J - 1e-11:
                stall += 1
                if stall > 8:
                    break
                continue

            res = self.line_search_dir(p, q)
            if res is None:
                stall += 1
                if stall > 12:
                    break
                continue

            pn, Jn = res
            if math.isfinite(Jn) and Jn < J - 1e-14:
                p = pn
                J = Jn
                c = self.conv(p)
                self.update_fft(p, J)
                if (it % 2 == 0) or (Jn < self.best_j - 1e-10):
                    self.exact_update(p)
                stall = 0
            else:
                stall += 1
                if stall > 12:
                    break

        self.exact_update(p)
        return p

    def pair_polish(self, p, stop, seed=777, max_moves=30000):
        p = self.normalize(p.copy())
        J = float(self.conv(p).max())
        self.update_fft(p, J)
        self.exact_update(p)
        rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        ratios = (0.0, 0.1, 0.25, 0.4, 0.5, 0.6, 0.75, 0.9, 1.0)
        moves = 0
        while time.monotonic() < stop - 3.0 and moves < max_moves:
            i = int(rng.integers(self.n))
            j = int(rng.integers(self.n))
            if i == j:
                continue
            oi = p[i]
            oj = p[j]
            tot = oi + oj
            if tot < 1e-14:
                moves += 1
                continue
            best_r = None
            best_j = J
            best_vals = None
            for r in ratios:
                p[i] = r * tot
                p[j] = (1.0 - r) * tot
                jv = float(self.conv(p).max())
                if jv < best_j - 1e-15:
                    best_j = jv
                    best_r = r
                    best_vals = (r * tot, (1.0 - r) * tot)
            if best_r is not None:
                p[i], p[j] = best_vals
                J = best_j
                self.update_fft(p, J)
                if moves % 250 == 0:
                    self.exact_update(p)
            else:
                p[i], p[j] = oi, oj
            moves += 1

        self.exact_update(p)
        return p


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = max(30.0, float(budget_s))
    hard_deadline = t0 + budget - 25.0
    target_score = 1.50274349

    raw_parent = _get_parent()
    if 1 <= raw_parent.size <= 2500:
        n_base = int(raw_parent.size)
    else:
        n_base = 384

    p_parent = _normalize(raw_parent, n_base)

    best_score = math.inf
    best_seq = None

    def try_set(arr):
        nonlocal best_score, best_seq
        if arr is None:
            return
        a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if a.size == 0:
            return
        a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
        a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
        s = float(a.sum())
        if not math.isfinite(s):
            a = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
            s = float(a.sum())
        if s < 0.01:
            if s > 1e-300:
                a = a / s
                s = 1.0
            else:
                a = np.ones(max(1, a.size), dtype=np.float64)
                s = float(a.sum())
        try:
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in a]))
        except Exception:
            sc = math.inf
        if math.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = sc
            best_seq = a.copy()

    try_set(np.ones(n_base) / n_base)
    try_set(p_parent)

    sol = _Opt(n_base)
    sol.exact_update(p_parent)

    inits = _make_inits(n_base, p_parent, int(seed) + 12345)
    scored = []
    for p in inits:
        pn = sol.normalize(p)
        c = sol.conv(pn)
        j = float(c.max()) if c.size else math.inf
        if math.isfinite(j):
            scored.append((j, pn))
            sol.update_fft(pn, j)
    scored.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])

    def similar(a, b):
        if a.size != b.size:
            return False
        return float(np.linalg.norm(a - b)) < 0.05

    tasks = []

    def add_task(p):
        p = sol.normalize(p)
        if any(similar(p, q) for q in tasks):
            return
        tasks.append(p)

    add_task(p_parent)
    add_task(p_parent[::-1])
    for _, p in scored:
        add_task(p)
        if len(tasks) >= 4:
            break

    T = max(40.0, budget - 60.0)
    base_alloc = T * 0.45
    polish_alloc = T * 0.20
    double_alloc = T * 0.35

    if base_alloc > 20.0 and time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 80.0:
        fracs = [0.50, 0.25, 0.15, 0.10][:len(tasks)]
        total_frac = sum(fracs) if fracs else 1.0
        if fracs:
            for frac in fracs:
                if time.monotonic() > hard_deadline - 80.0:
                    break
                alloc = base_alloc * (frac / total_frac)
                if alloc < 12.0:
                    break
                try:
                    sol.local_lse(tasks[0 if frac == fracs[0] else tasks[fracs.index(frac)]],
                                  alloc, hard_deadline)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                if sol.best_exact_p is not None:
                    try_set(sol.best_exact_p)
                if sol.best_p is not None:
                    try_set(sol.best_p)
                if best_score <= target_score + 1e-8:
                    break

    if time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 80.0 and polish_alloc > 20.0:
        p_best = sol.best_p if sol.best_p is not None else p_parent
        stop_lp = min(hard_deadline - 35.0, time.monotonic() + polish_alloc * 0.70)
        try:
            sol.lp_polish(p_best, stop_lp, seed=int(seed) + 9991, max_iter=90)
        except Exception:
            pass
        if sol.best_exact_p is not None:
            try_set(sol.best_exact_p)
        if sol.best_p is not None:
            try_set(sol.best_p)

        stop_pair = min(hard_deadline - 25.0, time.monotonic() + polish_alloc * 0.30)
        p_best = sol.best_p if sol.best_p is not None else p_best
        try:
            sol.pair_polish(p_best, stop_pair, seed=int(seed) + 8821, max_moves=25000)
        except Exception:
            pass
        if sol.best_exact_p is not None:
            try_set(sol.best_exact_p)
        if sol.best_p is not None:
            try_set(sol.best_p)

    if (
        time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 90.0
        and double_alloc > 70.0
        and best_score > target_score + 1e-9
    ):
        n2 = 2 * n_base
        if n2 <= 1024:
            init_src = best_seq if (best_seq is not None and best_seq.size == n_base) else p_parent
            init2 = _upsample(init_src, n2)
            sol2 = _Opt(n2)
            sol2.exact_update(init2)
            remaining = max(0.0, hard_deadline - time.monotonic() - 40.0)
            alloc2 = min(double_alloc, remaining)
            if alloc2 > 40.0:
                try:
                    sol2.local_lse(init2, alloc2 * 0.85, hard_deadline)
                except Exception:
                    pass
                if sol2.best_exact_p is not None:
                    try_set(sol2.best_exact_p)
                if sol2.best_p is not None:
                    try_set(sol2.best_p)

                stop_lp2 = min(hard_deadline - 35.0, time.monotonic() + alloc2 * 0.10)
                if n2 <= 850 and time.monotonic() < stop_lp2 - 10.0:
                    try:
                        p2 = sol2.best_p if sol2.best_p is not None else init2
                        sol2.lp_polish(p2, stop_lp2, seed=int(seed) + 31337, max_iter=25)
                    except Exception:
                        pass

                stop_pair2 = min(hard_deadline - 25.0, time.monotonic() + alloc2 * 0.05)
                if time.monotonic() < stop_pair2 - 10.0:
                    try:
                        p2 = sol2.best_p if sol2.best_p is not None else init2
                        sol2.pair_polish(p2, stop_pair2, seed=int(seed) + 55123, max_moves=12000)
                    except Exception:
                        pass

                if sol2.best_exact_p is not None:
                    try_set(sol2.best_exact_p)
                if sol2.best_p is not None:
                    try_set(sol2.best_p)

    final = best_seq
    if final is None:
        final = np.ones(n_base) / n_base
    final = np.asarray(final, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    final = np.where(np.isfinite(final), final, 0.0)
    final = np.clip(final, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if final.size == 0:
        final = np.ones(n_base) / n_base

    s = float(final.sum())
    if (not math.isfinite(s)) or s < 0.01:
        final = np.ones(n_base) / n_base

    try:
        sc_final = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in final]))
    except Exception:
        sc_final = math.inf

    if not math.isfinite(sc_final):
        for cand in (p_parent, np.ones(n_base) / n_base, np.ones(1)):
            try_set(cand)
        if best_seq is not None:
            final = best_seq
        else:
            final = np.ones(1)

    return [float(x) for x in final]
```
